- Action: QC mandatory matrix per route (employees + contracts + insurance/catalog); BLOCKER = empty UI + connection refused or 409 ? same-day NO-GO to PM.
- Outcome: Employees baseline valid only for that route; contracts NO-GO until Nest/proxy wiring and scope alignment.
- Evidence: `docs/qa/evidence/qc-hrm-embed-regression-20260522.md`; `hrm-embed-employees-fix-20260522.md`
- Reuse-tag: qc-hrm-embed-per-route-matrix

## 2026-05-04 | HRM iframe dialog portal lesson
- Context: Embedding a micro-FE in an iframe makes Radix `Portal` default to the iframe `document`, so modal backdrops do not cover the parent shell.
- Action: When same-origin, set Radix `Portal` `container` to `parent.document.body` and mirror the iframe?s stylesheet links into the parent head so Tailwind utility classes still apply to portaled nodes; use a high z-index vs shell rails; if `parent.document` throws, fall back to iframe-only and plan a `postMessage` host overlay for cross-origin.
- Outcome: Unified viewport overlay without forking every dialog call site when primitives are centralized.
- Evidence: `apps/web/hrm/src/lib/hrmDialogPortal.ts`, `docs/ecosystem/TECHSPEC.md` ?4.1
- Reuse-tag: fe-iframe-dialog-parent-portal

## 2026-04-29 11:59 | XEVN BA package handoff lesson
- For ecosystem-wide governance BRDs, publish one explicit BA matrix artifact that ties scope contracts, deterministic error codes, and requirement-to-test rows together; splitting these into separate unlinked notes increases implementation drift.
- TM transfer packets should embed mandatory handoff fields (`work_item_id`, `entry_criteria`, `exit_criteria`, `evidence_path`, `ack_status`) and a frozen error taxonomy so technical spec finalization remains auditable and test-ready.

## 2026-04-29 12:10 | TM consolidation to delivery packet lesson
- Technical spec handoff quality improved when architecture boundary rules, API scope precedence, and migration sequencing are packaged with deterministic stage-gate criteria in one artifact.
- A separate role allocation packet for Dev-BE, Dev-FE, QA, QC with strict execution order and explicit BLOCK/NOT GO conditions reduces gate ambiguity and release-risk interpretation gaps.

## 2026-04-29 08:50 | MAR D4 deterministic scope lesson
- For asset identity contracts, uniqueness scope must be explicitly frozen as `tenant_id + company_id + normalized_identity_key`; omitting scope causes FE/QA ambiguity and non-deterministic conflict handling.
- QA/QC handoff should include mandatory packet fields (`entry_criteria`, `exit_criteria`, `evidence_path`, `ack_status`) directly in artifacts, not only in chat summaries.

## 2026-04-29 09:05 | Asset registry module authority lesson
- Source of module authority in internal APIs must be JWT claim first, with headers treated as compatibility hints only.
- Canonicalization maps (`hrm/fleet/accounting`) should be normalized in one backend boundary to avoid FE/BE naming drift and reduce contract bugs.
- Add deterministic scope codes for required tenant/company fields when DTO validation may otherwise emit generic validation envelopes.

## 2026-04-29 09:40 | Asset registry ownership/identity lesson
- DB uniqueness constraints alone are insufficient for business determinism; service must translate `unique_violation` into stable business codes (`ASSET-REG-409`) with conflict-field details.
- If runtime allows multi-module writes by field, ownership persistence cannot store only first owner; keep policy catalog separate and track active owner + transfer history explicitly.
- Non-prod static-key fallback should never silently authorize mutation without module provenance; require explicit dev module assertion and environment allowlist.

## 2026-04-29 08:58 | FE deterministic conflict propagation lesson
- If FE envelope parsing drops backend `details`, deterministic business error contracts become non-actionable in UX even when backend behavior is correct.
- Keep conflict metadata (`conflictFields`, scoped tenant/company) through typed client errors and map canonical/snake-case field keys to inline form guidance for stable operator recovery.

## 2026-04-29 09:50 | MAR Stage 3 retest lesson
- Keep one executable live-path spec that asserts both `ASSET-REG-409` details (`conflictFields`, tenant/company scope) and cross-company allowance for same asset code to protect D4 from regression.
- FE conflict UX can regress silently without dedicated UI automation; at minimum preserve deterministic error-code mapping in `VehicleTypes` flow and gate release with build + targeted manual/automated UX evidence.

## 2026-04-29 12:15 | Ecosystem BRD determinism lesson
- Multi-company platform BRD must freeze scope model as `tenantId + companyId` across API, DB, error semantics, and test evidence; partial statements create downstream interpretation drift.
- When XBOS is source-of-truth for shared catalogs, downstream modules must be described as scoped consumers with local synchronized snapshots, not independent authorities.
- Governance ordering (`PM -> Dev -> QA -> BA -> SA -> QC`) should be documented with mandatory entry/exit/evidence fields to prevent non-auditable gate completion.

## 2026-06-16 | Android release ABI forcing can cause arm64 splash white screen
- Context: Mobile QA-device APK worked on x86_64 emulator but showed blank white screen from splash on physical arm64.
- Action: Compared APK libs per ABI and traced packaging script forcing `-PreactNativeArchitectures=x86_64` on Windows release builds; removed forcing and kept only explicit env override (`REACT_NATIVE_ARCHITECTURES`).
- Outcome: Rebuilt APK now ships full native libs for all ABIs (including `expo-modules-core`, `reanimated`, `rnscreens` on arm64), removing high-risk startup gap on physical devices.
- Evidence: `docs/qa/evidence/mob-white-screen-device-20260616-arm64-followup.md`
- Reuse-tag: android-multi-abi-release-no-forced-x86

## 2026-06-06 | HRM GET-by-id scope parity retest (J-HRM-05/06)
- Context: QA `P1-PHASE1-QA-CRUD-MATRIX-RETST` flagged scope_parity FAIL ? list returned ids but GET `/recruitment/requisitions/:id` and `/attendance/records/:recordId` returned 404 for `company_id=main`.

- Outcome: **J-HRM-05** **200** `HRM-REC-200` ? **J-HRM-06** **200** `HRM-ATT-200` portal + direct hrm-api; defects D-CRUDMAT-REC-RD-01 / D-CRUDMAT-ATT-RD-01 closed.
- Evidence: `docs/qa/evidence/p1-phase1-qa-crud-rd-retest-20260606.md`
- Reuse-tag: `scope-parity-get-by-id`, `j-hrm-05-06-retest`

## 2026-06-02 | P1-HRM-CRUD-QA-W1B-CONTRACT ? mutation gate needs post-action re-fetch

## 2026-05-03 02:20 | Post-MVP1 dependency hardening sequencing lesson
- Dependency scans should be triaged by actual runtime path and lockfile chain first; package-name alerts alone can overstate risk and cause unnecessary broad upgrades.
- For low-breakage delivery, split remediation into (A) immediate toolchain/chart-path refresh for active apps and (B) scoped runtime-risk package replacement/waiver workflow for legacy FE modules.
- Waivers must be time-bounded and role-owned (`owner`, `expiry`, `mitigation`, `next checkpoint`) with tighter SLA for production runtime vulnerabilities than for dev-only tooling findings.

## 2026-05-02 01:33 | MVP1 Batch3 BA lock lesson
- BA conformance locks are fastest when AC-001..AC-005 validation reuses one fixed chain (`SRS` + `TechSpec` + BA closure + QA report + governed source specs) and only refreshes verdict status in message bus.
- If no requirement or implementation delta exists, record explicit "no drift/no ambiguity" in BA handoff and keep verdict deterministic (`PASS_TO_SA`) to avoid unnecessary gate churn.

## 2026-05-02 01:30 | MVP1 Batch2 deep QA retest lesson
- Deep QA closure quality improves when the cycle combines full-suite confidence (`test/build/lint`) with targeted branch suites that directly exercise negative/mismatch/no-mutation/isolation behaviors, instead of relying on aggregate pass counts alone.
- For governed FE AC verification without dedicated FE tests, deterministic UX acceptance should explicitly confirm stable bracketed error codes and preserved `details` payload rendering in the active UI path, backed by reproducibility gates (`lint/build`).

## 2026-05-01 23:58 | MVP1 SA conformance rerun lesson
- SA conformance closure quality is stronger when each AC row is judged against both SRS/TechSpec architectural targets and BA/QA executable evidence in the same artifact.
- For AC-005-style FE deterministic checks, treat missing dedicated FE test script as an explicit watch item while still allowing gate pass when lint/build and deterministic source-path evidence are present.

## 2026-04-29 12:14 | P0 gate recovery dispatch lesson
- QC `NO-GO` recovery cycles are easier to execute when PM publishes one stage-gated dispatch artifact plus synchronized message-bus kickoff entries per role with explicit `work_item_id`, `needed_by`, and `ack_status`.
- Enforcing stage entry criteria tied to prior ACKed handoff packets prevents premature parallel starts and reduces retest churn for critical defect closure.

## 2026-05-01 22:40 | M4 retest evidence-completeness lesson
- Passing controller tests/builds is not sufficient for governance gate closure; checklist rows requiring DB immutability and cross-company negative isolation proofs must be evidenced explicitly in the same cycle packet.
- FE reproducibility gates can fail due to repository-level lint configuration drift; QA should treat unresolved lint/tooling reproducibility as a release-blocking evidence gap when `build/test` alone cannot prove deterministic delivery quality.

## 2026-05-01 22:58 | M4 rerun partial-closure lesson
- A rerun can close tooling blockers (e.g., FE lint/build reproducibility) while still remaining `BLOCKED` if higher-order deterministic evidence rows (DB immutability and explicit cross-company negative isolation) are not attached in the same packet.
- When Dev-BE adds guard-level "reject-before-service" tests, QA should still require explicit data-layer before/after proof for checklist items that are contractually defined at DB-verification level.

## 2026-05-01 23:10 | QA evidence acceptance lesson
- For checklist rows framed as DB immutability requirements, acceptance evidence should assert a concrete before/after persisted-state snapshot equality in an executable test, not only guard-level call-block assertions.
- For cross-company isolation closure, add a single-test execution command (`-t`) in the QA packet to prove the exact negative scenario was run and matched deterministic rejection code.

## 2026-05-01 22:35 | BA M5 traceability closure lesson
- For BA gate closure after QA reruns, freeze one explicit table that maps each corrected P0/P1 branch to requirement ref, implementation surface, and executed test command evidence in the same artifact.
- Promote to SA only when the message bus handoff packet and BA closure artifact use the same work-item IDs and deterministic verdict (`PASS_TO_SA`) to avoid governance drift.

## 2026-05-01 22:34 | SA M6 conformance handoff lesson
- SA gate closure quality improves when conformance notes explicitly separate gate-pass conformance from residual risk posture, then tie both to QC entry criteria in the same cycle packet.
- For claim-first/source-of-truth/deterministic checks, combine BA traceability row evidence with QA executable targeted tests (`-t` command proofs) to avoid architecture verdicts based only on narrative assertions.

## 2026-05-01 23:20 | M1 backend test determinism lesson
- Service-level unit tests that rely on strict mock-call order become brittle when schema bootstrap/query count changes; prefer SQL-intent based mock implementations to preserve deterministic behavior verification.
- For scope governance closure, enforce claim-first at controller boundary and assert reject-before-service-call in tests, then attach one handoff artifact that includes AC mapping and exact test/build command output excerpts.

## 2026-04-29 08:32 | QA gate before module wiring
- For new domains with ADR/backlog approved but endpoint wiring not ready, ship a contract-test harness in the existing backend test structure to validate deterministic business rules early.
- Mark release recommendation as `GO WITH CONDITIONS` until API+DB runtime evidence is attached from real controller/service/repository path.

## 2026-04-29 08:47 | Asset registry integration contract lesson
- Passing backend unit/contract tests do not guarantee web integration readiness when request/response naming conventions differ (`camelCase` API DTO/output vs `snake_case` FE client expectations).
- For internal APIs requiring `tenantId/companyId` scope and `x-module-code`, enforce a shared typed client contract and a smoke integration test from FE settings flow before gate escalation.

## 2026-05-01 23:18 | FE governed-path deterministic scope lesson
- For FE governed API calls, selected-company scope propagation should be centralized and reused in action handlers; fallback to static first-entity values can silently break multi-company isolation.
- Deterministic UX for QA replay improves when FE surfaces stable backend error `code` (and optional `details`) directly in user-visible branch messaging, especially for timeout/network/scope failure paths.

## 2026-05-01 23:35 | MVP1 QA immediate retest lesson
- A single M3 QA packet should combine executable command evidence (BE test/build + FE lint/build) with explicit AC mapping to targeted specs so BA intake can proceed without interpretation gaps.
- For AC-005 when FE test script is absent, treat `pnpm run --if-present test` clean exit as reproducibility evidence only, and rely on deterministic UX code-path checks plus lint/build for gate confidence.

## 2026-05-01 23:45 | MVP1 BA immediate-closure lesson
- After QA `PASS_TO_BA`, BA closure quality improves when AC rows are frozen in one artifact using deterministic requirement->implementation->test evidence columns, not split narrative notes.
- BA->SA message bus handoff should reuse the same work-item chain and verdict (`PASS_TO_SA`) declared in the BA artifact to prevent gate drift.

## 2026-05-02 00:45 | MVP1 backend scope-governance rerun lesson
- For multi-module backend reruns, keep one chain of evidence with full-suite pass (`test` + `build`) plus module-targeted reruns to produce deterministic PASS/FAIL by module instead of inferred status.
- Scope-governance acceptance should explicitly confirm reject branches (`tenantId/companyId` missing or mismatched) inside controller tests; generic happy-path PASS alone is insufficient for BA gate confidence.

## 2026-05-02 00:48 | BA reverify handoff lesson
- When QA adds module-level scope-governance coverage after an initial BA closure, BA can keep the same traceability artifact version if requirement-to-implementation mapping is unchanged and only evidence depth increased.
- Always append a fresh BA->SA message-bus packet that references the new module evidence and repeats explicit verdict (`PASS_TO_SA` or `BLOCKED`) to avoid stage-status ambiguity.

## 2026-05-02 00:48 | SA scope-governance rerun lesson
- For scope-governance expansion reruns, architecture re-validation should combine static boundary inspection (`resolveScopeContext` claim-first enforcement) with executable module-targeted controller tests to prove reject-before-service behavior remains deterministic.
- SA->QC handoff quality improves when the packet includes both code-level boundary evidence and command-level runtime evidence (`targeted tests + build`) in one traceable evidence path chain.

## 2026-05-02 01:35 | Batch1 deterministic reject-coverage lesson
- Claim-first scope guard code is insufficient as release evidence unless each governed controller suite explicitly covers both missing-scope and token/request mismatch branches with `service-not-called` assertions.
- In PowerShell CI/local runs, chain backend `test -> build` with explicit `$LASTEXITCODE` checks instead of `&&` to avoid false command failures unrelated to application quality.

## 2026-05-02 01:30 | FE governed-path scope bugfix lessons
- Do not fallback to first entity (`list[0]`) for governed scope resolution; this hides missing identity context and breaks multi-company isolation in UI actions.
- Action-level scope hints should prefer validated `row.company_id` and only then selected company hint; avoid loose `||` branching that treats empty strings as valid fallback triggers.
- Deterministic QA replay requires UI scope errors to include stable error codes (`SCOPE_*`) instead of message-only rendering.
- FE network branch messages should remain fixed (`NETWORK_ERROR`) while exposing raw cause in optional details for debugging to avoid nondeterministic UX text drift.

## 2026-05-02 02:02 | FE bundle splitting hygiene lesson
- Large FE chunks often come from route barrel imports that eagerly pull heavy modules (`charts/workflow`) into initial entry; prefer route-level `React.lazy` boundaries for feature pages.
- Pair lazy routes with explicit Vite `manualChunks` for known heavy vendors (e.g., `recharts`, `dagre`) to keep warning budgets stable and avoid future regressions after incremental feature additions.

## 2026-05-02 02:18 | Post-MVP1 FE hardening QA lesson
- For FE bundle hardening retests, require both reproducibility gate evidence (`lint`, `build`, `--if-present test`) and explicit confirmation that build output no longer emits the prior warning signature.
- Regression confidence is stronger when QA revalidates deterministic governed FE paths by source contracts (`error code + details propagation`, scope resolution guards) even if no dedicated FE test runner exists.

## 2026-05-03 02:16 | FE dependency hardening compatibility lesson
- ESLint major upgrades in Vite React repos can silently break reproducibility when projects still rely on `.eslintrc`; add an explicit `eslint.config.cjs` compatibility layer in the same change-set to keep `pnpm lint` deterministic.
- For transitive advisory closure on workspace-managed lockfiles, direct dependency bumps may not fully propagate until a targeted workspace refresh (`pnpm up -r <package>@<patched>`) is executed and verified via `pnpm why`.

## 2026-05-04 09:40 | Catalog sync readiness audit lesson
- For cross-system catalog sync, passing service/controller tests is not sufficient; migration/seed scripts must be aligned with the current scoped uniqueness model (`tenant_id + company_id + catalog_key`) or environment bring-up will drift.
- Keep one deterministic runbook chain (`migrate-status -> migrate-apply -> seed -> smoke`) and fail fast on missing DB env vars before startup to reduce ambiguous "API up but data path broken" states.
